Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on coding structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 553. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 138 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on coding structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 553. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 138. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any coding module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 539. The projective resolution has length at most 944 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 539 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the coding structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 944. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
